) holds. Thus we derive a lower bound at
y_{1}
from
x_{I}
by using
Corollary
3.4
:

f(t,x,v)\geq\mathbb{B}(m,0,\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0},\alpha^{\prime}_{0},|v_{I}|)\mathbf{1}_{B\left(y_{1},\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{m+1}}\right)\times B\left(\mathbb{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0},0,m,y_{1},x_{I}),\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{m+1}}\right)}(x,v)

for any
\mathbb{T}(\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0},0,m)\leq t\leq\min\big\{\Delta^{0},\tau^{\prime\prime}+\mathbb{D}(\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0},m)\big\}

and
m\geq\mathbb{M}(\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0},|v_{I}|,d(\overline{x_{I}y_{1}},\partial\Omega))
.
Notice that

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}(\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0},|v_{I}|,d(\overline{x_{I}y_{1}},\partial\Omega))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max\left\{1,\left\lceil\log_{\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}}\left(\frac{\frac{4d_{\Omega}}{\tau^{\prime\prime}}+|v_{I}|+1}{\Delta^{0}}\right)\right\rceil,\left\lceil\log_{2}{\frac{\Delta^{0}+1}{d(\overline{x_{I}y_{1}},\partial\Omega)}}\right\rceil\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max\left\{1,\left\lceil\log_{\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}}\left(\frac{\frac{4d_{\Omega}}{\tau^{\prime\prime}}+|v_{I}|+1}{\Delta^{0}}\right)\right\rceil,\left\lceil\log_{2}{\frac{\Delta^{0}+1}{\min\{d(x_{I},\partial\Omega),\frac{d}{10}\}}}\right\rceil\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathfrak{n}_{1},

which implies that as long as
m\geq\mathfrak{n}_{1}
, we have
m\geq\mathbb{M}(\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0},|v_{I}|,d(\overline{x_{I}y_{1}},\partial\Omega))
, and this finishes the case for
j=1
.

Step 2: Induction: propagation of the lower bound from
y_{k}
to
y_{k+1}
.

Now, suppose that the case
j=k
holds, i.e.,

(3.26)

\begin{split}&f(t,x,v)\geq\ \mathbb{B}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k}(\widetilde{m}),\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0},\alpha^{\prime}_{0},|v_{I}|)\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\mathbf{1}_{B\left(y_{k},\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\widetilde{m}+1+\sum_{l=1}^{k-1}(\mathfrak{n}_{l}+1)}}\right)\times B\left(\mathbb{V}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k}(\widetilde{m}),\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\widetilde{m}+1+\sum_{l=1}^{k-1}(\mathfrak{n}_{l}+1)}}\right)}(x,v),\end{split}

for any

\begin{split}&\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k}(\widetilde{m}),\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})\leq t\\
\leq&\min\{\mathbb{U}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k}(\widetilde{m}),\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),\tau^{\prime\prime}+\mathbb{D}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k}(\widetilde{m}),\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})\}\end{split}

and
\widetilde{m}\geq\mathfrak{n}_{k}
.

By taking
\widetilde{m}=\mathfrak{n}_{k}
, we use
Corollary
3.4
again by considering

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=\mathbb{B}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0},\alpha^{\prime}_{0},|v_{I}|),

\displaystyle\tau

\displaystyle=\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),

\displaystyle\Delta_{1}

\displaystyle=\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),

\displaystyle\Delta_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\min\{\mathbb{U}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),\tau^{\prime\prime}+\mathbb{D}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})\}-\tau^{\prime\prime},

\displaystyle x^{\prime}

\displaystyle=y_{k},\,y=y_{k+1},

\displaystyle v^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{V}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),

\displaystyle\Delta_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\sum_{l=1}^{k}(\mathfrak{n}_{l}+1)}}.

We notice that
by
Remark
3.4
,
we have
\Delta_{1}>0
and
\Delta_{1}^{\prime}>0
.

Also, all the assumptions of
Corollary
3.4
are satisfied again since

\displaystyle B\left(y_{k},\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\sum_{l=1}^{k}(\mathfrak{n}_{l}+1)}}\right)\subset B\left(y_{k},\frac{d}{10}\right)\subset\Omega,

\overline{y_{k}y_{k+1}}\in\Omega
,
\Delta_{1}\leq\Delta^{0}\leq\frac{1}{2}
, and

\displaystyle\Delta_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\min\{\mathbb{U}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),\tau^{\prime\prime}+\mathbb{D}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})\}-\tau^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{U}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})-\tau^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{U}_{1}-\tau^{\prime\prime}\leq\Delta^{0}-\tau^{\prime\prime}\leq\Delta^{0}\leq\frac{1}{2}.

Here, we recall that
\mathbb{U}_{1}=\Delta^{0}
.
Hence, by noticing that

\displaystyle|\mathbb{V}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2|y_{k}-y_{k-1}|}{\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})-\mathbb{T}_{k-1}(\mathfrak{N}_{k-1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})-\mathbb{T}_{k-1}(\mathfrak{N}_{k-1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})},

we have

(3.27)

\begin{split}&f(t,x,v)\\
\geq&\ \mathbb{B}\Bigg(m,\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),\\
&\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\sum_{l=1}^{k}(\mathfrak{n}_{l}+1)}},\mathbb{B}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0},\alpha^{\prime}_{0},|v_{I}|),|\mathbb{V}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})|\Bigg)\\
&\mathbf{1}_{B\left(y_{k+1},\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{m+1+\sum_{l=1}^{k}(\mathfrak{n}_{l}+1)}}\right)\times B\left(\mathbb{V}_{k+1}(\mathfrak{N}_{k+1}(m),\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{m+1+\sum_{l=1}^{k}(\mathfrak{n}_{l}+1)}}\right)}(x,v)\\
\geq&\mathbb{B}_{k+1}(\mathfrak{N}_{k+1}(m),\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0},\alpha^{\prime}_{0},|v_{I}|)\\
&\mathbf{1}_{B\left(y_{k+1},\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{m+1+\sum_{l=1}^{k}(\mathfrak{n}_{l}+1)}}\right)\times B\left(\mathbb{V}_{k+1}(\mathfrak{N}_{k+1}(m),\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{m+1+\sum_{l=1}^{k}(\mathfrak{n}_{l}+1)}}\right)}(x,v),\end{split}

for any

\displaystyle m\geq\mathbb{M}(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\sum_{l=1}^{k}(\mathfrak{n}_{l}+1)}},|\mathbb{V}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})|,d(\overline{y_{k}y_{k+1}},\partial\Omega)).

We also notice that

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\Bigg(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),

\displaystyle\hskip 17.07182pt\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\sum_{l=1}^{k}(\mathfrak{n}_{l}+1)}},|\mathbb{V}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})|,d(\overline{y_{k}y_{k+1}},\partial\Omega)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max\Bigg\{1,\left\lceil\log_{\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}}\left(\frac{\frac{4d_{\Omega}}{\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})}+\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{N}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})-\mathbb{T}_{k-1}(\mathfrak{N}_{k-1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})}+1}{\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\sum_{l=1}^{k}(\mathfrak{n}_{l}+1)}}}\right)\right\rceil

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt\left\lceil\log_{2}{\frac{\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\sum_{l=1}^{k}(\mathfrak{n}_{l}+1)}}+1}{\frac{d}{10}}}\right\rceil\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathfrak{n}_{k+1}.

Hence,
m\geq\mathfrak{n}_{k+1}
is sufficient for (
3.27
) to hold.

Step 3: Conclusion.

Finally, by induction, the estimate holds for
j=1,...,\operatorname{Conn}_{d}(x_{I},y^{0}_{i})
and we conclude the proof of
Proposition
3.5
by selecting
j=\operatorname{Conn}_{d}(x_{I},y^{0}_{i})
and defining

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\max_{1\leq j\leq\operatorname{Conn}_{d}(\Omega)}\left\{\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{\mathbb{T}_{j}(\mathfrak{N}_{j},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})-\mathbb{T}_{j-1}(\mathfrak{N}_{j-1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\min_{1\leq j\leq\operatorname{Conn}_{d}(\Omega)}\{\mathbb{B}_{j}(\mathfrak{N}_{j},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0},\alpha^{\prime}_{0},|v_{I}|)\},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{d}_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\sum_{i=1}^{\operatorname{Conn}_{d}(\Omega)}(\mathfrak{n}_{i}+1)}}\leq\frac{d}{10},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{d}_{T}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\min\bigg\{\frac{\Delta^{0}-\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2},\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{\operatorname{Conn}_{d}(\Omega)}(\mathfrak{N}_{\operatorname{Conn}_{d}(\Omega)},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt\mathbb{U}_{\operatorname{Conn}_{d}(\Omega)}(\mathfrak{N}_{\operatorname{Conn}_{d}(\Omega)},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})-\tau^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{D}_{\operatorname{Conn}_{d}(\Omega)}(\mathfrak{N}_{\operatorname{Conn}_{d}(\Omega)},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})\bigg\},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\min\{\mathfrak{d}_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}_{T}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\}\leq\frac{d}{10},

\displaystyle v_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\mathbb{V}_{\operatorname{Conn}_{d}(x_{I},y^{0}_{i})}(\mathfrak{N}_{\operatorname{Conn}_{d}(x_{I},y^{0}_{i})},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}).

∎

Remark 3.4
.

Notice that for any
j\geq 1
, we have

(3.28)

\tau^{\prime\prime}>\mathbb{T}_{j+1}(\mathfrak{M}_{j+1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})>\mathbb{T}_{j}(\mathfrak{M}_{j},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})\geq\frac{\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2}

(with possibly equality when
j=1
in the lower bound
\mathbb{T}_{j}\geq\frac{\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2}
)

(3.29)

0<\mathbb{D}_{j+1}(\mathfrak{M}_{j+1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})<\mathbb{D}_{j}(\mathfrak{M}_{j},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),

and

(3.30)

\tau^{\prime\prime}<\mathbb{U}_{j+1}(\mathfrak{M}_{j+1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})\leq\mathbb{U}_{j}(\mathfrak{M}_{j},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),

for
\mathfrak{m}_{1}\geq\mathfrak{n}_{1}
,
\mathfrak{m}_{i}\geq 0
for
i\geq 2
.

Proof.

We prove it by induction on
j
. For
j=1
, assume
\mathfrak{m}_{1}\geq\mathfrak{n}_{1}
. We notice that

(3.31)

\frac{\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2}\leq\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})=\mathbb{T}(\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0},0,\mathfrak{m}_{1})=\max\left\{\frac{\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2},\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tau^{\prime\prime}\Delta^{0}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{1}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\tau^{\prime\prime})}\right\}<\tau^{\prime\prime}

and that

(3.32)

\begin{split}&\mathbb{T}_{2}((\mathfrak{m}_{1},\mathfrak{m}_{2}),\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})=\mathbb{T}(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{1}+1}},\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),\mathfrak{m}_{2})\\
=&\max\left\{\frac{\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2}+\frac{\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})}{2},\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{1}+1}}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{2}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})))}\right\}.\end{split}

By (
3.31
), we have

\frac{\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2}+\frac{\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})}{2}>\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}).

Next, we notice that the following inequality

(3.33)

\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{1}+1}}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{2}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})))}>\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})

is equivalent to the inequality below:

(3.34)

\Delta^{0}<2^{\mathfrak{m}_{1}+\mathfrak{m}_{2}+3}(4d_{\Omega}+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))).

We notice that by
Lemma
2.1
, we have
d_{\Omega}\geq d
. Indeed, we have

x_{i}^{0}-2dn(x_{i}^{0}),\,x_{i}^{0}-\frac{1}{2}dn(x_{i}^{0})\in\Omega,\,\left|x_{i}^{0}-2dn(x_{i}^{0})-(x_{i}^{0}-\frac{1}{2}dn(x_{i}^{0}))\right|=\frac{3d}{2}.

Notice that

(3.35)

2^{\mathfrak{m}_{1}}\geq\frac{\Delta^{0}+1}{\min\{d(x_{I},\partial\Omega),\frac{d}{10}\}}.

Here we use the fact that

\mathfrak{m}_{1}\geq\mathfrak{n}_{1}=\max\left\{1,\left\lceil\log_{\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}}\left(\frac{\frac{4d_{\Omega}}{\tau^{\prime\prime}}+|v_{I}|+1}{\Delta^{0}}\right)\right\rceil,\left\lceil\log_{2}{\frac{\Delta^{0}+1}{\min\{d(x_{I},\partial\Omega),\frac{d}{10}\}}}\right\rceil\right\}.

As a result, we deduce that

(3.36)

\begin{split}&2^{\mathfrak{m}_{1}+\mathfrak{m}_{2}+3}(4d_{\Omega}+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})))\\
\geq&\frac{\Delta^{0}+1}{\min\{d(x_{I},\partial\Omega),\frac{d}{10}\}}2^{\mathfrak{m}_{2}+3}(4d_{\Omega}+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})))\\
\geq&\frac{32(\Delta^{0}+1)d}{\min\{d(x_{I},\partial\Omega),\frac{d}{10}\}}\\
>&\frac{d(\Delta^{0}+1)}{\frac{d}{10}}\\
>&\Delta^{0}.\end{split}

Hence, we conclude that
\mathbb{T}_{2}((\mathfrak{m}_{1},\mathfrak{m}_{2}),\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})>\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})
.

For the number
\mathbb{D}_{j}(\mathfrak{M}_{j},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{D}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})=\mathbb{D}(\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0},\mathfrak{m}_{1})=\frac{\tau^{\prime\prime}\Delta^{0}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{1}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\tau^{\prime\prime})}>0.

We also notice that

\displaystyle\mathbb{D}_{2}((\mathfrak{m}_{1},\mathfrak{m}_{2}),\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})=\mathbb{D}(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{1}+1}},\mathfrak{m}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{1}+1}}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{2}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))\Delta^{0}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{1}+\mathfrak{m}_{2}+3}(4d_{\Omega}+\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\frac{\tau^{\prime\prime}\Delta^{0}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{1}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\tau^{\prime\prime})}=\mathbb{D}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}).

For
\mathbb{U}_{j}(\mathfrak{M}_{j},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{U}_{1}:=\Delta^{0}>\tau^{\prime\prime},

\displaystyle\mathbb{U}_{2}((\mathfrak{m}_{1},\mathfrak{m}_{2}),\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})=\min\{\mathbb{U}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime}+\mathbb{D}_{1}(\mathfrak{m}_{1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})\}\leq\mathbb{U}_{1}.

So we finished the case
j=1
.

Suppose that (
3.28
), (
3.29
), (
3.30
) hold for
j=k
, we have

\begin{split}&(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))(4d_{\Omega}+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k-1}(\mathfrak{M}_{k-1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})))\\
<&2^{\mathfrak{m}_{k+1}+1}(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k-1}(\mathfrak{M}_{k-1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))(4d_{\Omega}+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))),\end{split}

from which we deduce that

\begin{split}&\frac{(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))\Delta^{0}}{2^{1+\sum_{l=1}^{k+1}(\mathfrak{m}_{l}+1)}(4d_{\Omega}+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})))}\\
<&\frac{(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k-1}(\mathfrak{M}_{k-1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))\Delta^{0}}{2^{1+\sum_{l=1}^{k}(\mathfrak{m}_{l}+1)}(4d_{\Omega}+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k-1}(\mathfrak{M}_{k-1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})))}.\end{split}

Hence, we have

(3.37)

\begin{split}&\mathbb{T}_{k+1}(\mathfrak{M}_{k+1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})\\
=&\mathbb{T}(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\sum_{l=1}^{k}(\mathfrak{m}_{l}+1)}},\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),\mathfrak{m}_{k+1})\\
=&\max\begin{Bmatrix}\frac{\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2}+\frac{\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})}{2},\\
\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\sum_{l=1}^{k}(\mathfrak{m}_{l}+1)}}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{k+1}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})))}\end{Bmatrix}\\
=&\max\Bigg\{\frac{\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2}+\frac{\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})}{2},\\
&\hskip 34.14322pt\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\sum_{l=1}^{k}(\mathfrak{m}_{l}+1)}}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{k+1}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})))}\Bigg\}\\
=&\max\Bigg\{\frac{\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2}+\frac{\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})}{2},\\
&\hskip 34.14322pt\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))\Delta^{0}}{2^{1+\sum_{l=1}^{k+1}(\mathfrak{m}_{l}+1)}(4d_{\Omega}+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})))}\Bigg\}\\
>&\max\Bigg\{\frac{\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2}+\frac{\mathbb{T}_{k-1}(\mathfrak{M}_{k-1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})}{2},\\
&\hskip 34.14322pt\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k-1}(\mathfrak{M}_{k-1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))\Delta^{0}}{2^{1+\sum_{l=1}^{k}(\mathfrak{m}_{l}+1)}(4d_{\Omega}+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k-1}(\mathfrak{M}_{k-1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})))}\Bigg\}\\
=&\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}).\end{split}

Also, we notice that by induction hypothesis

(3.38)

\begin{split}&\mathbb{T}_{k+1}(\mathfrak{M}_{k+1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})\\
=&\max\Bigg\{\frac{\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2}+\frac{\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})}{2},\\
&\hskip 34.14322pt\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))\Delta^{0}}{2^{1+\sum_{l=1}^{k+1}(\mathfrak{m}_{l}+1)}(4d_{\Omega}+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})))}\Bigg\}\\
<&\tau^{\prime\prime}.\end{split}

Next, we notice that

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle<\mathbb{D}_{k+1}(\mathfrak{M}_{k+1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{D}(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\sum_{l=1}^{k}(\mathfrak{m}_{l}+1)}},\mathfrak{m}_{k+1})

\displaystyle=\frac{(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\sum_{l=1}^{k}(\mathfrak{m}_{l}+1)}}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{k+1}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})))}

\displaystyle<\frac{(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k-1}(\mathfrak{M}_{k-1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))\frac{\Delta^{0}}{2^{\sum_{l=1}^{k-1}(\mathfrak{m}_{l}+1)}}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{k}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{k-1}(\mathfrak{M}_{k-1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})))}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{D}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),

and that

\displaystyle\tau^{\prime\prime}<

\displaystyle\mathbb{U}_{k+1}(\mathfrak{M}_{k+1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\min\{\mathbb{U}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),\tau^{\prime\prime}+\mathbb{D}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})\}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\min\{\mathbb{U}_{k-1}(\mathfrak{M}_{k-1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}),\tau^{\prime\prime}+\mathbb{D}_{k-1}(\mathfrak{M}_{k-1},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{U}_{k}(\mathfrak{M}_{k},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}).

Hence, we show that (
3.28
), (
3.29
), (
3.30
) holds for
j=k+1
, and we conclude the proof of the statement of
Remark
3.4
.

∎

Remark 3.5
.

The numbers
\{\mathfrak{n}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{\operatorname{Conn}_{d}(\Omega)}
and
\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
do not depend on the choice of zigzag.

Proposition 3.6
.

Suppose that
\Omega\subset\mathbb{R}^{3}
satisfies
Assumption A
and that the collision kernel
B
satisfies
Assumption B
with
\nu<0
,
\alpha\in[0,1]
. Let
f(t,x,v)
be a continuous mild solution to (
1.1
)–(
1.3
). We consider
0<\tau^{\prime\prime}<\Delta^{0}
, where
\Delta^{0}
is given in
Proposition
3.1
. Then, there exist
0<\delta_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime})=\delta_{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\leq\frac{d}{56}
,
\delta_{T}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
,
a_{0}(\tau^{\prime\prime})>0
, which depend on
\Omega
,
M
,
E_{f}
, and a modulus of continuity of
f_{0}
(and
L_{f,p}
if
\gamma<0
); and
\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\geq 2
, which depends on
\tau^{\prime\prime}
,
\Omega
,
M
, and
E_{f}
(and
L_{f,p}
if
\gamma<0
), such that
\delta_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\geq\delta_{T}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
and that for all
N\in\mathbb{N}
, there exist
\mathcal{N}(N,\tau^{\prime\prime})
in
\mathbb{N}
,
\{x_{i}\}_{i=1}^{\mathcal{N}(N,\tau^{\prime\prime})}
in
\Omega
and
\{\overline{v}_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\}_{i=1}^{\mathcal{N}(N,\tau^{\prime\prime})}
in
B(0,\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))
, such that

(3.39)

\overline{\Omega}\subset\underset{1\leq i\leq\mathcal{N}(N,\tau^{\prime\prime})}{\bigcup}B\left(x_{i},\frac{\delta_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{N}}\right),

(3.40)

f(t,x,v)\geq a_{0}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\mathbf{1}_{B(\overline{v}_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\delta_{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}(v)

for any
1\leq i\leq\mathcal{N}(N,\tau^{\prime\prime})
, and any
(t,x,v)\in[\tau^{\prime\prime},\tau^{\prime\prime}+\delta_{T}(\tau^{\prime\prime})]\times[B(x_{i},\delta_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))\cap\overline{\Omega}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
.

Proof.

Given
0<\tau^{\prime\prime}<\Delta^{0}
, we choose
0<d<\min\{1,\delta\}
and notice that by
Proposition
3.5
there exist
\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime})>0
,
\frac{d}{10}\geq\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})>0
,
\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
,
\{v_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\}_{i=1}^{m_{1}+m_{2}}\in B(0,\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))
such that

(3.41)

f(t,x,v)\geq\ \mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\mathbf{1}_{B(y^{0}_{i},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))\times B(v_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}(v),\ \forall t\in[\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})],

for any
1\leq i\leq m_{1}+m_{2}
.

Notice that, by the definition and
Remark
3.4
, we have

(3.42)

\begin{split}\tau^{\prime\prime}&>\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\geq\tau^{\prime\prime}-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbb{T}_{\operatorname{Conn}_{d}(\Omega)}(\mathfrak{N}_{\operatorname{Conn}_{d}(\Omega)},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0}))\\
&=\mathbb{T}_{\operatorname{Conn}_{d}(\Omega)}(\mathfrak{N}_{\operatorname{Conn}_{d}(\Omega)},\tau^{\prime\prime},\Delta^{0})>\frac{\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2},\end{split}

(3.43)

\tau^{\prime\prime}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\leq\tau^{\prime\prime}+\frac{\Delta^{0}-\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2}=\frac{\Delta^{0}+\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2}<\Delta^{0}.

Now, we define

(3.44)

\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}:=\max\left\{\mathbb{M}\left(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\frac{d}{16}\right),\left\lceil\log_{\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}}\left(\frac{\frac{4d_{\Omega}}{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}+\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+1}{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}\right)\right\rceil\right\}.

Notice that

r_{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))=\left(\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}\right)^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}}\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\geq\frac{4d_{\Omega}}{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}+\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+1.

We also define

(3.45)

\begin{split}\delta_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime})=\delta_{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\min\left\{\frac{1}{4}\min\limits_{1\leq i\leq m_{1}+m_{2}}d(y_{i}^{0},\partial\Omega),\frac{d}{56},\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+2}},\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}\right\}.\end{split}

Given
N\in\mathbb{N}
, we notice that by compactness of
\overline{\Omega}
, there exists
\{x_{i}\}_{i=1}^{\mathcal{N}(N,\tau^{\prime\prime})}\in\Omega
such that
\overline{\Omega}\subset\underset{1\leq i\leq\mathcal{N}(N,\tau^{\prime\prime})}{\bigcup}B(x_{i},\frac{\delta_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{N}})
.
Given
1\leq i\leq\mathcal{N}(N,\tau^{\prime\prime})
, we consider two cases.

1. Point near the boundary
: If
d(x_{i},\partial\Omega)\leq\frac{d}{7}
, then there exists
x^{\prime}_{i}\in\partial\Omega
such that
|x^{\prime}_{i}-x_{i}|\leq\frac{d}{6}
. Also, by (
2.1
), there exists
k(i)\in\{1,2,...,m_{1}\}
such that
|x_{k(i)}^{0}-x^{\prime}_{i}|\leq\frac{d}{8}
, so that
|x_{i}-x_{k(i)}^{0}|\leq|x_{i}-x_{i}^{\prime}|+|x_{i}^{\prime}-x_{k(i)}^{0}|\leq\frac{7}{24}d
.

We consider

(3.46)

\displaystyle T^{\prime\prime}:=\max\left\{\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right\},

\displaystyle\overline{v}_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\frac{2(x_{i}-y^{0}_{k(i)})}{T^{\prime\prime}-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}=\frac{2(x_{i}-y^{0}_{k(i)})}{\max\left\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right\}}.

\mathfrak{R}^{1}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{\max\left\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right\}}

Here, we recall the definition of
y^{0}_{j}
in
Lemma
2.1
.
As long as
x\in B(x_{i},\min\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+1}},\frac{d}{56}\})\cap\Omega
,
v\in B\left(\overline{v}_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\min\left\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+1}(t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})))},1\right\}\right)
,
T^{\prime\prime}\leq t\leq T^{\prime\prime}+\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+1}\left(\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{T^{\prime\prime}-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}+1\right)}
, we have

(3.47)

x-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v\in B\left(y^{0}_{k(i)},\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}}}\right).

Furthermore, by definition of
\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}
in (
3.44
), we have

(3.48)

r_{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))\geq\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+\frac{4d_{\Omega}}{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}+1\geq|v_{k(i)}(\tau^{\prime\prime})|+\left|\frac{2(x_{i}-y^{0}_{k(i)})}{T^{\prime\prime}-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right|+1\geq|v_{k(i)}(\tau^{\prime\prime})|+|v|,

which implies

(3.49)

v\in B(v_{k(i)}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),r_{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))).

Here,
\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
is defined in
Proposition
3.5
.
Next, notice that

(3.50)

|x-x^{0}_{k(i)}|\leq|x-x_{i}|+|x_{i}-x^{0}_{k(i)}|\leq\frac{d}{56}+\frac{7d}{24}<\frac{d}{3},

so we have
x\in B(x^{0}_{k(i)},\frac{d}{3})
.
Now, we parametrize
x
and
x-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v
as below:

(3.51)

x=x^{0}_{k(i)}+\widetilde{x}_{1}e^{1}_{k(i)}+\widetilde{x}_{2}e^{2}_{k(i)}-\widetilde{x}_{3}n(x^{0}_{k(i)}),

(3.52)

x-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v=x^{0}_{k(i)}+\overline{x}_{1}e^{1}_{k(i)}+\overline{x}_{2}e^{2}_{k(i)}-\overline{x}_{3}n(x^{0}_{k(i)}).

We notice by (
3.50
) that
\widetilde{x}_{3}<\frac{d}{3}
.

Since we have (notice that
\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\leq\frac{d}{8}
)

x-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v\in B(y^{0}_{k(i)},\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}}})\subset B(y^{0}_{k(i)},\frac{d}{8}),

we deduce that
\overline{x}_{3}>\frac{3d}{4}-\frac{d}{8}
.

As a result, we have

\displaystyle|x-[x-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v]|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|x-x^{0}_{k(i)}|+|x^{0}_{k(i)}-y^{0}_{k(i)}|+|y^{0}_{k(i)}-[x-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v]|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{d}{3}+\frac{3d}{4}+\frac{d}{8}=\frac{29d}{24},

which implies that

(3.53)

\begin{split}&n(x^{0}_{k(i)})\cdot\frac{v}{|v|}\\
=&n(x^{0}_{k(i)})\cdot\frac{x-[x-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v]}{|x-[x-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v]|}\\
\geq&n(x^{0}_{k(i)})\cdot\frac{[(\widetilde{x}_{1}-\overline{x}_{1})e^{1}_{k(i)}+(\widetilde{x}_{2}-\overline{x}_{2})e^{2}_{k(i)}-(\widetilde{x}_{3}-\overline{x}_{3})n(x^{0}_{k(i)})]}{\frac{29d}{24}}\\
=&\frac{\overline{x}_{3}-\widetilde{x}_{3}}{\frac{29d}{24}}\\
\geq&\frac{\frac{3d}{4}-\frac{d}{8}-\frac{d}{3}}{\frac{29d}{24}}=\frac{7}{29}.\end{split}

Hence, by using the method as before, we have

(3.54)

\left\{\,x-sv\middle|\ 0\leq s\leq\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}\,\right\}\subset\Omega.

We also notice that

(3.55)

\begin{split}&T^{\prime\prime}+\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+1}\left(\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{T^{\prime\prime}-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}+1\right)}\\
\geq&\max\left\{\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right\}+\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+1}\left(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)}\\
\geq&\tau^{\prime\prime}+\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+2}\left(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)}.\end{split}

Since
B(y^{0}_{k(i)},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))\subset B(y^{0}_{k(i)},\frac{d}{8})\subset\Omega
by
Lemma
2.2
, from (
4.2
), we can apply
Proposition
3.2
with
\tau=\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
,
\Delta_{1}=2\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\ \Delta_{2}=\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
,
x^{\prime}=y^{0}_{k(i)}
, and
v^{\prime}=v_{k(i)}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
.

Hence, we have for
\tau^{\prime\prime}\leq t\leq T^{\prime\prime}+\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+1}\left(\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{T^{\prime\prime}-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}+1\right)}
(see
Remark
3.2
and the fact that
T^{\prime\prime}+\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+1}\left(\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{T^{\prime\prime}-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}+1\right)}\leq\tau^{\prime\prime}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
)

(3.56)

\begin{split}&f\left(\frac{t+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2},x-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v,v\right)\\
\geq&\alpha_{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}}\left(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)\\
&\mathbf{1}_{B\left(y^{0}_{k(i)},\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}}}\right)\times B(v_{k(i)}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),r_{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})))}\left(x-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v,v\right),\end{split}

which implies by the Duhamel formula that for any
x\in B(x_{i},\min\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+1}},\frac{d}{56}\})\cap\Omega
,
v\in B(\overline{v}_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\min\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+1}(t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})))},1\})
,
\tau^{\prime\prime}\leq t\leq T^{\prime\prime}+\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+1}\left(\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{T^{\prime\prime}-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}+1\right)}
, we have

(3.57)

\begin{split}&f(t,x,v)\\
\geq&f\left(\frac{t+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2},x-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v,v\right)e^{-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}C_{L}\left\langle\mathfrak{R}^{1}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+1\right\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}\\
\geq&\alpha_{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}}\left(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)\\
&\mathbf{1}_{B\left(y^{0}_{k(i)},\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}}}\right)\times B(v_{k(i)}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),r_{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})))}\left(x-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v,v\right)e^{-C_{L}\left\langle\mathfrak{R}^{1}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+1\right\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}\\
\geq&\alpha_{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}}\left(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)e^{-C_{L}\left\langle\mathfrak{R}^{1}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+1\right\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}.\end{split}

Here, the last line is from (
3.47
) and (
3.49
).
Now, we define

\delta_{T,1}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}.

Notice that
\delta_{T,1}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\leq\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
, and we see by (
3.55
) that

\tau^{\prime\prime}+\delta_{T,1}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\leq T^{\prime\prime}+\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+1}\left(\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{T^{\prime\prime}-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}+1\right)}.

As a result, we deduce from (
3.57
) that for all

(x,v)\in\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
and
\tau^{\prime\prime}\leq t\leq\tau^{\prime\prime}+\delta_{T,1}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
, we have

\displaystyle f(t,x,v)\geq\alpha_{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}}\left(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)e^{-C_{L}\left\langle\mathfrak{R}^{1}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+1\right\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{B(x_{i},\min\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+1}},\frac{d}{56}\})\times B(\overline{v}_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\min\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+1}(t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})))},1\})}(x,v)

\displaystyle\geq\alpha_{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}}\left(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)e^{-C_{L}\left\langle\mathfrak{R}^{1}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+1\right\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\mathbf{1}_{B(x_{i},\delta_{X})\times B(\overline{v}_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\delta_{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}(x,v),

where we used (
3.45
) to derive that
\delta_{X}\leq\max\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+1}},\frac{d}{56}\}
.
In the final line we used the fact that

\displaystyle\min\left\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+1}(t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})))},1\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\min\left\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+1}((\tau^{\prime\prime}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})))},1\right\}\geq\min\left\{\frac{1}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+2}},1\right\}.

2. Point far from the boundary.

If
d(x_{i},\partial\Omega)>\frac{d}{7}
, by
Lemma
2.2
, we can find
m_{1}+1\leq k(i)\leq m_{1}+m_{2}
such that
x_{i}\in B(y^{0}_{k(i)},\frac{d}{16})
, which implies
\overline{y^{0}_{k(i)}x_{i}}\subset B(y^{0}_{k(i)},\frac{d}{16})\subset\Omega-\Omega_{\frac{d}{16}}
.

We can use
Corollary
3.4
, since by (
4.2
), (
3.22
) is satisfied with
\tau=\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
,
\Delta_{1}=\Delta_{1}^{\prime}=\Delta_{2}=\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
,
x^{\prime}=y^{0}_{k(i)}
,
y=x_{i}
,
v^{\prime}=v_{k(i)}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
. Notice as before that all the assumptions of
Corollary
3.4
are satisfied by the fact that
B(y^{0}_{k(i)},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))\subset B(y^{0}_{k(i)},\frac{d}{8})\subset\Omega
and that
\overline{y^{0}_{k(i)}x_{i}}\subset B\left(y^{0}_{k(i)},\frac{d}{16}\right)\subset\Omega
.

Thus, we have

(3.58)

\begin{split}&f(t,x,v)\\
&\geq\mathbb{B}(\widetilde{m},\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),|v_{k(i)}(\tau^{\prime\prime})|)\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt\times\mathbf{1}_{B(x_{i},\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\widetilde{m}+1}})\times B\left(\mathbb{V}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\widetilde{m},x_{i},y^{0}_{k(i)}),\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\widetilde{m}+1}}\right)}(x,v).\end{split}

for
\widetilde{m}\geq\mathbb{M}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),|v_{k(i)}(\tau^{\prime\prime})|,d(\overline{y^{0}_{k(i)}x_{i}},\partial\Omega))
,
(x,v)\in\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
and
\mathbb{T}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\widetilde{m})\leq t\leq\min\{\tau^{\prime\prime}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}+\mathbb{D}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\widetilde{m}))\}
.
Next, we define
\delta_{T,2}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\min\{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathbb{D}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime})\}
.
We notice that

(3.59)

\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}=\mathbb{M}\left(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\frac{d}{16}\right)\geq\mathbb{M}\left(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),|v_{k(i)}(\tau^{\prime\prime})|,d\left(\overline{y^{0}_{k(i)}x_{i}},\partial\Omega\right)\right).

As a result, by (
3.58
), we have for
(x,v)\in\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
and
\mathbb{T}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime})\leq t\leq\min\{\tau^{\prime\prime}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}+\mathbb{D}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}))\}=\tau^{\prime\prime}+\delta_{T,2}(\tau^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle f(t,x,v)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathbb{B}(\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime},\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),|v_{k(i)}(\tau^{\prime\prime})|)

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{B(x_{i},\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+1}})\times B\left(\mathbb{V}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime},x_{i},y^{0}_{k(i)}),\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+1}}\right)}(x,v)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathbb{B}(\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime},\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),|v_{k(i)}(\tau^{\prime\prime})|)

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{B(x_{i},\delta_{X})\times B\left(\mathbb{V}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime},x_{i},y^{0}_{k(i)}),\delta_{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)}(x,v)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathbb{B}(\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime},\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{B(x_{i},\delta_{X})\times B\left(\mathbb{V}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime},x_{i},y^{0}_{k(i)}),\delta_{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)}(x,v),

where we used (
3.45
).
We now define

(3.60)

\overline{v}_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\mathbb{V}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime},x_{i},y^{0}_{k(i)})=\frac{2(x_{i}-y^{0}_{k(i)})}{\max\left\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right\}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}^{2}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{\max\left\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right\}}

and finish the case by noticing that

\mathbb{T}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime})<\tau^{\prime\prime}

and that

|\overline{v}_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime})|\leq\mathfrak{R}^{2}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}).

We conclude the proof by defining

(3.61)

\delta_{T}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\min\{\delta_{T,1}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\delta_{T,2}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\delta_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\},

(3.62)

\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\max\{\mathfrak{R}^{1}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{R}^{2}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),2\},

and

\begin{split}&a_{0}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\min\bigg\{\alpha_{\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime}}\left(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)e^{-C_{L}\left\langle\mathfrak{R}^{1}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+1\right\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}},\\
&\hskip 71.13188pt\mathbb{B}(\mathfrak{n}^{\prime\prime},\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))\bigg\}.\end{split}

∎

4.
Maxwellian bound for the cut-off case for non-fully specular reflection condition

In this section, we consider the lower bound problem in the cut-off case with a non-fully specular boundary condition.

Proposition 4.1
.

Suppose that
\Omega\subset\mathbb{R}^{3}
satisfies
Assumption A
and that the collision kernel
B
satisfies
Assumption B
with
\alpha\in[0,1]
. We consider a continuous mild solution
f(t,x,v)
of (
1.1
)–(
1.3
) . Given
\tau^{\prime\prime}\in(0,\Delta^{0})
with
\Delta^{0}>0
given in
Proposition
3.1
, there exist
b(\tau^{\prime\prime})
,
\delta_{T,I}(\tau^{\prime\prime})>0
, which depends on
\Omega,M,E_{f}
, and the modulus of continuity of
f_{0}
, such that for
t\in[\tau^{\prime\prime},\tau^{\prime\prime}+\delta_{T,I}(\tau^{\prime\prime})]
,

(4.1)

\forall x\in\partial\Omega,\ \int_{v_{*}\cdot n(x)>0}f(t,x,v_{*})(v_{*}\cdot n(x))\,dv_{*}>b(\tau^{\prime\prime}).

Remark 4.1
.

Although the estimate remains valid for
\alpha=1
, in our analysis, we only employ it for
\alpha\in[0,1)
.

Proof.

Given
0<\tau^{\prime\prime}<\Delta^{0}
, we choose
0<d<\min\{1,\delta\}
and notice that by
Proposition
3.5
there exist
\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime})>0
,
\frac{d}{10}\geq\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})>0
,
\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
,
\{v_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\}_{i=1}^{m_{1}+m_{2}}\in B(0,\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))
such that

(4.2)

f(t,x,v)\geq\ \mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\mathbf{1}_{B(y^{0}_{i},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))\times B(v_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}(x,v),\ \forall t\in[\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})],

for any
1\leq i\leq m_{1}+m_{2}
.
Given
x_{\partial}\in\partial\Omega
, by
Lemma
2.1
, there exists
1\leq i\leq m_{1}
such that
x_{\partial}\in B(x^{0}_{i},\frac{d}{8})
.

Then, we proceed with the same method as in
Proposition
3.6
, this time we propagate the lower bound from
x^{0}_{i}
to
x_{\partial}

We define

(4.3)

\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}:=\max\left\{1,\left\lceil\log_{\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}}\left(\frac{\frac{4d_{\Omega}}{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}+\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+1}{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}\right)\right\rceil\right\},

and define

(4.4)

\displaystyle T_{\partial}:=\max\left\{\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right\},

(4.5)

\displaystyle v_{x_{\partial}}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\frac{2(x_{\partial}-y^{0}_{i})}{T_{\partial}-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}=\frac{2(x_{\partial}-y^{0}_{i})}{\max\left\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right\}},

(4.6)

\displaystyle R^{\partial}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{\max\left\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right\}}.

Notice that, by the definition of notation of
R^{\partial}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
, we have

(4.7)

|v_{x_{\partial}}(\tau^{\prime\prime})|\leq R^{\partial}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}).

As long as
v\in B\left(v_{x_{\partial}}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\min\left\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+1}(t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})))},1\right\}\right)
,
T_{\partial}\leq t\leq T_{\partial}+\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+1}\left(\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{T_{\partial}-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}+1\right)}
, we have

(4.8)

\begin{split}&\left|x_{\partial}-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v-y^{0}_{i}\right|<\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+1}}.\end{split}

Furthermore, by the definition of
\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}
in (
4.3
), we have

(4.9)

r_{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))\geq\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+\frac{4d_{\Omega}}{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}+1\geq|v_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime})|+\left|\frac{2(x_{\partial}-y^{0}_{i})}{T_{\partial}-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right|+1\geq|v_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime})|+|v|,

which implies
v\in B(v_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),r_{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})))
. Here, we recall
\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
again in
Proposition
3.5
.
Next, notice that

(4.10)

|x_{\partial}-x^{0}_{i}|\leq\frac{d}{8},

and that

(4.11)

x_{\partial}-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v\in B(y^{0}_{i},\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+1}})\subset B(y^{0}_{i},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))\subset B(y^{0}_{i},\frac{d}{8})

by the fact that
\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\leq\frac{d}{8}
.
As before, we note that the segment
\left\{x_{\partial}-sv|\ 0\leq s\leq\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}\right\}
between
x_{\partial}
and
x_{\partial}-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v\in B\left(y^{0}_{i},\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}}}\right)
also lies within
\Omega
.
We also notice that

(4.12)

\begin{split}&T_{\partial}+\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+1}\left(\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{T_{\partial}-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}+1\right)}\\
\geq&\max\left\{\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right\}+\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+1}\left(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)}\\
\geq&\tau^{\prime\prime}+\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+2}\left(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)}.\end{split}

Then, using
Proposition
3.2
(note that as before by (
4.2
), the assumption (
3.9
) holds with
\tau=\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
,
\Delta_{1}=2\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\ \Delta_{2}=\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
,
x^{\prime}=y^{0}_{i}
, and
v^{\prime}=v_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
, we also have
B(y^{0}_{i},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))\subset B(y^{0}_{i},\frac{d}{8})\subset\Omega
by
Lemma
2.2
), we have for
\tau^{\prime\prime}\leq t\leq T_{\partial}+\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+1}\left(\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{T_{\partial}-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}+1\right)}
(see
Remark
3.2
and the fact that
T_{\partial}+\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+1}\left(\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{T_{\partial}-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}+1\right)}\leq\tau^{\prime\prime}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
)

(4.13)

\begin{split}&f\left(\frac{t+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2},x_{\partial}-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v,v\right)\\
\geq&\alpha_{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}}\left(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)\\
&\mathbf{1}_{B\left(y^{0}_{i},\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}}}\right)\times B\left(v_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),r_{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))\right)}\left(x_{\partial}-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v,v\right),\end{split}

which implies by the Duhamel formula that for any
v\in B(v_{x_{\partial}}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\min\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+1}(t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})))},1\})
,
\tau^{\prime\prime}\leq t\leq T_{\partial}+\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+1}\left(\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{T_{\partial}-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}+1\right)}
, we have (notice that by (
4.7
), we have
|v|\leq|v_{x_{\partial}}(\tau^{\prime\prime})|+1\leq R^{\partial}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+1
)

(4.14)

\begin{split}&f(t,x_{\partial},v)\\
\geq&f\left(\frac{t+(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2},x_{\partial}-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v,v\right)e^{-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}C_{L}\left\langle R^{\partial}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+1\right\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}\\
\geq&\alpha_{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}}\left(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\tau^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)\\
&\mathbf{1}_{B\left(y^{0}_{i},\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}}}\right)\times B(v_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),r_{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})))}\left(x_{\partial}-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v,v\right)e^{-C_{L}\left\langle R^{\partial}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+1\right\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}.\end{split}

Next, we recall (
4.8
) and (
4.9
):

(4.15)

x_{\partial}-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v\in B\left(y^{0}_{i},\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}}}\right),v\in B(v_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),r_{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))).

So, we have

(4.16)

\begin{split}&\alpha_{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}}(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\frac{2\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))\\
&\mathbf{1}_{B\left(y^{0}_{i},\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}}}\right)\times B(v_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),r_{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}}(\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})))}(x_{\partial}-\frac{t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}{2}v,v)e^{-C_{L}\left\langle R^{\partial}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+1\right\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}\\
\geq&\alpha_{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}}(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\frac{2\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))e^{-C_{L}\left\langle R^{\partial}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+1\right\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}.\end{split}

Now, we define
\delta_{T,I}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}
(notice as before that
\delta_{T,I}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\leq\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
), and notice by (
4.12
) that

(4.17)

T_{\partial}+\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+1}\left(\frac{2d_{\Omega}}{T_{\partial}-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}+1\right)}\geq\tau^{\prime\prime}+\delta_{T,I}(\tau^{\prime\prime}).

As a result, we have by (
4.14
) that for all

(x,v)\in\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
and
\tau^{\prime\prime}\leq t\leq\tau^{\prime\prime}+\delta_{T,I}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
,

\displaystyle f(t,x_{\partial},v)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\alpha_{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}}(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\frac{2\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))e^{-C_{L}\left\langle R^{\partial}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+1\right\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{B(v_{x_{\partial}}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\min\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+1}(t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})))},1\})}(v)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\alpha_{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}}(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\frac{2\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))e^{-C_{L}\left\langle R^{\partial}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+1\right\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}\mathbf{1}_{B(v_{x_{\partial}}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\frac{1}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+2}})}(v).

In the final line, we use the fact again that

\min\left\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+1}(t-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})))},1\right\}\geq\min\left\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+1}((\tau^{\prime\prime}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))-(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})))},1\right\}\geq\frac{1}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+2}}.

Next, we define

\displaystyle A^{\prime}:=\alpha_{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}}(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\frac{2\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{B}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\mathfrak{R}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))e^{-C_{L}\left\langle R^{\partial}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+1\right\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}},

\displaystyle\delta_{V,I}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\frac{1}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+2}}.

We conclude that

(4.18)

\begin{split}&f(t,x_{\partial},v)\geq A^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{B(v_{x_{\partial}}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\delta_{V,I}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}(v),\\
\end{split}

for
\tau^{\prime\prime}\leq t\leq\tau^{\prime\prime}+\delta_{T,I}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
. Here we recall as in (
4.5
) that
v_{x_{\partial}}(\tau^{\prime\prime})=\frac{2(x_{\partial}-y^{0}_{i})}{\max\left\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right\}}
.

Next, by parameterizing

x_{\partial}=x_{i}^{0}+x_{\partial,1}e^{1}_{i}+x_{\partial,2}e^{2}_{i}-\phi_{i}(x_{\partial,1},x_{\partial,2})n(x_{i}^{0}),

we find that

(4.19)

\begin{split}&v_{x_{\partial}}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\cdot n(x_{\partial})\\
=&\frac{2}{\max\left\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right\}}[(x_{i}^{0}-y_{i}^{0})+(x_{\partial}-x_{i}^{0})]\cdot n(x_{\partial})\\
\geq&\frac{2}{\max\left\{\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2},\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})-\frac{\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{2^{\mathfrak{m}_{\partial}+2}(4d_{\Omega}+\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right\}\sqrt{|\nabla\phi_{i}(x_{\partial,1},x_{\partial,2})|^{2}+1}}\\
&\times\left[\left(0,0,-\frac{3d}{4}\right)\cdot(\partial_{x}\phi_{i}(x_{\partial,1},x_{\partial,2}),\partial_{y}\phi_{i}(x_{\partial,1},x_{\partial,2}),-1)-\left|(x_{\partial}-x_{i}^{0})\right|\right]\\
\geq&\frac{5d}{8\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}.\end{split}

Then, we have

v\cdot n(x_{\partial})\geq\frac{5d}{16\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})},\ \forall v\in B\left(v_{x_{\partial}}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\frac{5d}{16\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}\right).

Hence, for
t\in[\tau^{\prime\prime},\tau^{\prime\prime}+\delta_{T,I}(\tau^{\prime\prime})]
,
x_{\partial}\in\partial\Omega
,

(4.20)

\begin{split}&\int_{v_{*}\cdot n(x_{\partial})>0}f(t,x_{\partial},v_{*})(v_{*}\cdot n(x_{\partial}))\,dv_{*}\\
\geq&\int_{B\left(v_{x_{\partial}}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\min\{\delta_{V,I}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\frac{5d}{16\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}\}\right)}A^{\prime}\frac{5d}{16\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}\,dv_{*}\\
\geq&A^{\prime}\frac{5d}{16\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}\frac{4\pi(\min\{\delta_{V,I}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\frac{5d}{16\mathfrak{d}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}\})^{3}}{3}\\
=:&b(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\end{split}

and we finish the proof.

∎

The rest of the proof of existence of lower bound is exactly the same as in Briant’s paper
[
1
]
(from Prop. 3.9, pp. 21–25). Before we introduce the next proposition, we define some constants here:

Definition 4.1
.

Given
\tau^{\prime\prime},\tau^{\prime}>0
,
N\in\mathbb{N}
,
0\leq\alpha<1
,
\delta_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
,
\delta_{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
,
\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
as in
Prop.
3.6
and a sequence
\{\xi_{n}\}_{n=1}^{\infty}
satisfying
0\leq\xi_{n}\leq\frac{1}{4}
for
n\in\mathbb{N}
, we define the following numbers:

(4.21)

r^{\prime\prime}_{n}:=\begin{cases}\delta_{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\ n=0\\
\sqrt{2}(1-\xi_{n})r^{\prime\prime}_{n-1},\ n\geq 1,\end{cases}

(4.22)

b_{n,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime}):=\begin{cases}\min\{b(\tau^{\prime\prime}),a_{0}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\}&,\ n=0\\
\min\begin{Bmatrix}(1-\alpha)b(\tau^{\prime\prime})e^{-C_{L}(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\langle\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{1}\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}\\
\times\frac{1}{2\pi T_{B}^{2}}e^{-\frac{(\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{1})^{2}}{2T_{B}}},\\
(\min(a_{0}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),b(\tau^{\prime})))^{2}C_{Q}r_{0}^{{}^{\prime\prime}3+\gamma}\xi^{\frac{1}{2}}_{1}\\
\times\frac{\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2^{2}(\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{1})}e^{-C_{L}\frac{(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\langle\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{1}\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}{2(\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{1})}}\end{Bmatrix}&,\ n=1\\
(1-\alpha)b(\tau^{\prime\prime})e^{-C_{L}(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\langle\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{n}\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}\frac{1}{2\pi T_{B}^{2}}e^{-\frac{(\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{n})^{2}}{2T_{B}}}&,\ n\geq 2,\end{cases}

where
b(\tau^{\prime\prime})
is introduced in
Prop.
4.1
, and

(4.23)

a_{n,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime}):=\begin{cases}a_{0}(\tau^{\prime\prime})&,\ n=0\\
(\min(a_{n-1,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime}),b_{n-1,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime})))^{2}C_{Q}r_{n-1}^{{}^{\prime\prime}3+\gamma}\xi^{\frac{1}{2}}_{n}\\
\times\frac{\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2^{n+1}(\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{n})}e^{-C_{L}\frac{(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\langle\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{n}\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}{2^{n}(\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{n})}}&,\ n\geq 1,\end{cases}

where
a_{0}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
is defined in
Proposition
3.6
.

We now introduce the following proposition.

Proposition 4.2
.

Suppose that
\Omega\subset\mathbb{R}^{3}
satisfies
Assumption A
and that the collision kernel
B
satisfies
Assumption B
with
\nu<0
. Let
f
be the continuous mild solution of (
1.1
)–(
1.3
) with
0\leq\alpha<1
. We fix
\tau^{\prime\prime}\in(0,\Delta^{0})
, where
\Delta^{0}>0
is given in
Proposition
3.1
.
Then, there exist
\delta_{T,D}(\tau^{\prime\prime})>0
, which depends on
\Omega,M,E_{f}
, and the modulus of continuity of
f_{0}
, such that for every
\tau^{\prime}\in(\tau^{\prime\prime},\tau^{\prime\prime}+\delta_{T,D}(\tau^{\prime\prime})]
and every
N\in\mathbb{N}
, for each
0\leq n\leq N
and each
1\leq i\leq\mathcal{N}(N,\tau^{\prime\prime})
, we have

(4.24)

\begin{split}&\ \ \ \ \ f(t,x,v)\geq\min\left(a_{n,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime}),b_{n,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime})\right)\mathbf{1}_{B(\overline{v}_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),r^{\prime\prime}_{n})}(v),\\
&\forall(t,x)\in\left[\tau^{\prime\prime}+(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\left(1-\frac{1}{2^{n+1}(\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{n})}\right),\tau^{\prime}\right]\times\left[B\left(x_{i},\frac{\delta_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{n}}\right)\cap\overline{\Omega}\right],\end{split}

where
x_{i}
and
\overline{v}_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
are defined in
Proposition
3.6
and depend on
N
.
Here, we recall that the notation
\delta_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
,
\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
,
\mathcal{N}(N,\tau^{\prime\prime})
are defined in
Proposition
3.6
and that
a_{n,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime})>0,\,b_{n,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime})>0
and
r^{\prime\prime}_{n}
are defined in
Definition
4.1
.

Proof.

The proof follows the same induction-on-
n
scheme as Proposition 3.9 of
[
1
]
. Notice that we have
\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\geq 1
in
Proposition
3.6
. Then, we define (we recall that the notion
\delta_{T,I}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
provided in
Proposition
4.1
)

(4.25)

\delta_{T,D}(\tau^{\prime\prime}):=\min\{\delta_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\delta_{T}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),\delta_{T,I}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\}

and pick
\tau^{\prime}\in(\tau^{\prime\prime},\tau^{\prime\prime}+\delta_{T,D}(\tau^{\prime\prime})]
. This choice ensures
\delta_{T,D}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\leq\delta_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime})

(for the interior displacement),
\delta_{T,D}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\leq\delta_{T}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
(for the boundary integral) and
\delta_{T,D}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\leq\delta_{T,I}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
(for the time slab).
The proof uses an induction on
n
. The case
n=0
is a direct consequence of
Proposition
3.6
.

We now assume that the case
n=k
holds. Given
1\leq i\leq\mathcal{N}(N,\tau^{\prime\prime})
, consider

(4.26)

\begin{split}&(t,x,v)\in\left[\tau^{\prime\prime}+(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\left(1-\frac{1}{2^{k+2}(r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right),\tau^{\prime}\right]\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times\left[B\left(x_{i},\frac{\delta_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{k+1}}\right)\cap\overline{\Omega}\right]\times B\left(\overline{v}_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}\right).\end{split}

We generate the announced lower bound depending on whether the characteristic line
\{X_{s,t}(x,v)\}_{\tau^{\prime\prime}\leq s\leq t}
touches the boundary or not.

First case:
X_{s,t}(x,v)\in\overline{\Omega}
for
\tau^{\prime\prime}\leq s\leq t
.

We use the Duhamel formula (
1.12
) and apply
Lemma
3.1
to deduce that

(4.27)

\begin{split}&f(t,x,v)\\
\geq&\int_{\tau^{\prime\prime}+(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\left(1-\frac{1}{2^{k+1}\left(r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)}\right)}^{\tau^{\prime\prime}+(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\left(1-\frac{1}{2^{k+2}\left(r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)}\right)}e^{-C_{L}(t-s)\left\langle r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}\\
&Q^{+}[f(s,X_{s,t}(x,v),\cdot),f(s,X_{s,t}(x,v),\cdot)](v)\,ds.\end{split}

Next, we notice that in the assumption that

(4.28)

\tau^{\prime}\in(\tau^{\prime\prime},\tau^{\prime\prime}+\delta_{T,D}(\tau^{\prime\prime})],

for any
(t,x,v)
which satisfies (
4.26
), for all

(4.29)

\begin{split}&\tau^{\prime\prime}+(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\left(1-\frac{1}{2^{k+1}(r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right)\leq s\\
\leq&\tau^{\prime\prime}+(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\left(1-\frac{1}{2^{k+2}(r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right),\end{split}

we have

(4.30)

\begin{split}|X_{s,t}(x,v)-x_{i}|\leq\frac{\delta_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{k}}.\end{split}

Indeed, we have

(4.31)

|X_{s,t}(x,v)-x_{i}|\leq|X_{s,t}(x,v)-x|+|x-x_{i}|\leq|v|(t-s)+\frac{\delta_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{k+1}}.

Plus, we have

(4.32)

|v|\leq r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+|\overline{v}_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime})|\leq r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),

t-s\leq\tau^{\prime}-\left[\tau^{\prime\prime}+(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\left(1-\frac{1}{2^{k+1}(r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\right)\right]=\frac{\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2^{k+1}(r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}.

Hence, we have (we also use (
4.28
) and (
4.25
))

(4.33)

\begin{split}&|X_{s,t}(x,v)-x_{i}|\leq\frac{\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2^{k+1}}+\frac{\delta_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{k+1}}\leq\frac{\delta_{T,D}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{k+1}}+\frac{\delta_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{k+1}}\leq\frac{\delta_{X}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}{2^{k}}.\end{split}

Since
r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}(\tau^{\prime\prime})>r^{\prime\prime}_{k}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
, we have

\tau^{\prime\prime}+(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\left(1-\frac{1}{2^{k+1}\left(r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)}\right)>\tau^{\prime\prime}+(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\left(1-\frac{1}{2^{k+1}\left(r^{\prime\prime}_{k}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)}\right).

Thus we can apply the induction assumption and deduce that

(4.34)

f(s,X_{s,t}(x,v),w)\geq\min\left(a_{k,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime}),b_{k,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime})\right)\mathbf{1}_{B(\overline{v}_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),r^{\prime\prime}_{k})}(w),

for any
w\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
,
s
satisfying (
4.29
),
i=1,...,\mathcal{N}(N,\tau^{\prime\prime})
, and
(t,x,v)
satisfying (
4.26
).
Thus, we deduce from (
4.34
) and
Lemma
3.2
that

(4.35)

\begin{split}&f(t,x,v)\\
\geq&\int_{\tau^{\prime\prime}+(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\left(1-\frac{1}{2^{k+1}\left(r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)}\right)}^{\tau^{\prime\prime}+(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\left(1-\frac{1}{2^{k+2}\left(r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)}\right)}\min\left(a_{k,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime}),b_{k,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime})\right)^{2}\\
&\times e^{-C_{L}(t-s)\langle r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}C_{Q}(r^{\prime\prime}_{k})^{3+\gamma}\xi_{k+1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{1}_{B\left(\overline{v}_{i}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}\right)}(v)\,ds\\
\geq&\int_{\tau^{\prime\prime}+(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\left(1-\frac{1}{2^{k+1}\left(r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)}\right)}^{\tau^{\prime\prime}+(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\left(1-\frac{1}{2^{k+2}\left(r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)}\right)}\min\left(a_{k,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime}),b_{k,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime})\right)^{2}\\
&\times e^{-C_{L}(t-s)\langle r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}C_{Q}(r^{\prime\prime}_{k})^{3+\gamma}\xi_{k+1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\,ds\\
\geq&\min\left(a_{k,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime}),b_{k,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime})\right)^{2}C_{Q}(r^{\prime\prime}_{k})^{3+\gamma}\xi_{k+1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2^{k+2}(r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}\\
&\times e^{\frac{-(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})C_{L}\langle r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}{2^{k+1}(r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))}}\\
=&a_{k+1,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime})\geq\min\left(a_{k+1,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime}),b_{k+1,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime})\right).\end{split}

Hence, we showed that (
4.24
) holds for
n=k+1
when
X_{s,t}(x,v)\in\Omega
for
\tau^{\prime\prime}\leq s\leq t
.

Second case:
X_{s,t}(x,v)\notin\overline{\Omega}
for some
s\in[\tau^{\prime\prime},t]
.

First, we observe that by definition of
t_{\partial}(x,v)
in
Definition
1.1
, we have
t_{\partial}(x,v)\leq t-s\leq t-\tau^{\prime\prime}\leq\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime}
. Next, we notice by (
4.25
) that
\tau^{\prime}\in(\tau^{\prime\prime},\tau^{\prime\prime}+\delta_{T,I}(\tau^{\prime\prime})]
. Then, since
\alpha<1
, the diffuse part yields a strictly positive contribution, thus we use (
1.13
),
Proposition
4.1
, and
Lemma
3.1
to derive that for any
(t,x,v)
satisfying (
4.26
), we have

(4.36)

\begin{split}&f(t,x,v)\\
\geq&(1-\alpha)\left(\int_{w\cdot n(X_{t-t_{\partial}(x,v),t}(x,v))>0}f(t,X_{t-t_{\partial}(x,v),t}(x,v),w)(w\cdot n(X_{t-t_{\partial}(x,v),t}(x,v)))dw\right)\\
&\frac{1}{2\pi T_{B}^{2}}e^{-\frac{|v|^{2}}{2T_{B}}}\exp\left[-\int_{t-t_{\partial}(x,v)}^{t}L[f(s,X_{s,t}(x,v),\cdot)](v)\,ds\right]\\
\geq&(1-\alpha)b(\tau^{\prime\prime})\frac{1}{2\pi T_{B}^{2}}e^{-\frac{|v|^{2}}{2T_{B}}}e^{-t_{\partial}(x,v)C_{L}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}\\
\geq&(1-\alpha)b(\tau^{\prime\prime})\frac{1}{2\pi T_{B}^{2}}e^{-\frac{(\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1})^{2}}{2T_{B}}}e^{-(\tau^{\prime}-\tau^{\prime\prime})C_{L}{\left\langle\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{k+1}\right\rangle}^{\gamma^{+}}}\\
\geq&b_{k+1,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime})\geq\min\left(a_{k+1,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime}),b_{k+1,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau^{\prime})\right),\end{split}

which shows that (
4.24
) holds for
n=k+1
in the case when the backward characteristic
s\mapsto X_{s,t}(x,v)
touches the boundary.

In conclusion, we showed that (
4.24
) holds for
n=k+1
and by induction that (
4.24
) holds for
0\leq n\leq N
.
∎

Finally, we end the proof of
Theorem
1.1
for case
\alpha\in[0,1)
. We pick
0<\xi<\frac{1}{4}
and setting
\xi_{n}:=\xi^{n}
, by which we can deduce that (we recall
r_{n}^{\prime\prime}
from (
4.21
))

(4.37)

c_{r}(\xi)2^{\frac{n}{2}}\leq r^{\prime\prime}_{n}=\delta_{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime})2^{\frac{n}{2}}\Pi_{i=1}^{n}(1-\xi^{i})\leq\delta_{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime})2^{\frac{n}{2}},

where
c_{r}(\xi):=\delta_{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\Pi_{i=1}^{\infty}(1-\xi^{i})>0
.

We first show that given
\tau_{2}\in\left[\tau^{\prime\prime}+\min\left\{\frac{\delta_{T,D}(\tau^{\prime\prime})-\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2},\tau^{\prime\prime}\right\},\tau^{\prime\prime}+\delta_{T,D}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right]
there exists
\mathcal{A}>0
such that

(4.38)

f(\tau_{2},x,v)\geq\mathcal{A}^{2^{n}},\ \forall n\in\mathbb{N},\ (x,v)\in\overline{\Omega}\times B(0,c_{r}(\xi)2^{\frac{n}{2}}),

to get a lower maxwellian lower bound of a continuous mild solution of (
1.1
)–(
1.3
).

We will use the proof from
[
1
,
11
]
to show the existence of
\mathcal{A}_{1}>0
and
\mathcal{A}_{2}>0
such that

(4.39)

b_{n,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau_{2})\geq\mathcal{A}_{1}^{2^{n}},\ a_{n,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau_{2})\geq\mathcal{A}_{2}^{2^{n}},

for any
0<\tau^{\prime\prime}<\tau_{2}\leq\tau^{\prime\prime}+\delta_{T,D}(\tau^{\prime\prime})
.

In this article, we only show the sketch of the proof. For details, see
[
1
,
11
]
.
First, we observe that for
n\geq 2
(we recall that
\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\geq 1
)

\displaystyle b_{n,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\alpha)b(\tau^{\prime\prime})e^{-C_{L}(\tau_{2}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\langle\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{n}\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}\frac{1}{2\pi T_{B}^{2}}e^{-\frac{(\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{n})^{2}}{2T_{B}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\alpha)b(\tau^{\prime\prime})e^{-C_{L}(\tau_{2}-\tau^{\prime\prime})(1+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{n})^{\gamma^{+}}}\frac{1}{2\pi T_{B}^{2}}e^{-\frac{(\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{n})^{2}}{2T_{B}}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle(1-\alpha)b(\tau^{\prime\prime})\frac{1}{2\pi T_{B}^{2}}e^{-C_{L}\tau_{2}\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{\gamma^{+}}(2+r^{\prime\prime}_{n})^{2}}e^{-\frac{(\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{n})^{2}}{2T_{B}}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle(1-\alpha)b(\tau^{\prime\prime})\frac{1}{2\pi T_{B}^{2}}e^{-8C_{L}\tau_{2}\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{\gamma^{+}}}e^{-\frac{\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{T_{B}}}e^{-\left((2C_{L}\tau_{2}\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{\gamma^{+}}+\frac{1}{T_{B}})(\delta_{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))^{2}2^{n}\right)}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle C_{1}e^{-\left((2C_{L}\tau_{2}\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{\gamma^{+}}+\frac{1}{T_{B}})(\delta_{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))^{2}2^{n}\right)},

where we defined
C_{1}:=(1-\alpha)b(\tau^{\prime\prime})\frac{1}{2\pi T_{B}^{2}}e^{-8C_{L}\tau_{2}\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{\gamma^{+}}}e^{-\frac{\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{2}}{T_{B}}}
.

By defining

\mathcal{A}_{1}(\tau_{2}):=\begin{cases}\min\left\{b_{0,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau_{2}),\sqrt{b_{1,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau_{2})},e^{-\left((2C_{L}\tau_{2}\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{\gamma^{+}}+\frac{1}{T_{B}})(\delta_{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))^{2}\right)}\right\},\ C_{1}\geq 1\\
\min\left\{b_{0,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau_{2}),\sqrt{b_{1,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau_{2})},C_{1}e^{-\left((2C_{L}\tau_{2}\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{\gamma^{+}}+\frac{1}{T_{B}})(\delta_{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))^{2}\right)}\right\},\ C_{1}<1,\end{cases}

we conclude that for
n\in\mathbb{N}

(4.40)

b_{n,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau_{2})\geq\mathcal{A}_{1}^{2^{n}}(\tau_{2}).

For
a_{n,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau_{2})
, we also recall the definition (for
n\geq 1
):

\displaystyle a_{n,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau_{2})=(\min(a_{n-1,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau_{2}),b_{n-1,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau_{2})))^{2}C_{Q}r_{n-1}^{{}^{\prime\prime}3+\gamma}\xi^{\frac{n}{2}}\frac{\tau_{2}-\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2^{n+1}(\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{n})}

\displaystyle\hskip 54.06006pt\times e^{-C_{L}\frac{(\tau_{2}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\langle\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{n}\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}{2^{n}(\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{n})}}.

We notice that for
n\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{(\tau_{2}-\tau^{\prime\prime})\langle\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{n}\rangle^{\gamma^{+}}}{2^{n+1}(\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{n})}\leq\frac{\tau_{2}(1+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))^{\gamma^{+}}}{\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}+\frac{\tau_{2}(\delta_{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))^{\gamma^{+}}}{\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}.

By defining

C_{2}:=C_{Q}\min\left\{\frac{\delta_{T,D}(\tau^{\prime\prime})-\tau^{\prime\prime}}{2},\tau^{\prime\prime}\right\}e^{-C_{L}\left(\frac{\tau_{2}(1+\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))^{\gamma^{+}}}{\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}+\frac{\tau_{2}(\delta_{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime}))^{\gamma^{+}}}{\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})}\right)},

we have the following inequality:

\displaystyle a_{n,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau_{2})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle C_{2}(\min(a_{n-1,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau_{2}),b_{n-1,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau_{2})))^{2}r_{n-1}^{{}^{\prime\prime}3+\gamma}\xi^{\frac{n}{2}}\frac{1}{2^{n+1}(\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+r^{\prime\prime}_{n})}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle C_{2}(\min(a_{n-1,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau_{2}),b_{n-1,\tau^{\prime\prime}}(\tau_{2})))^{2}(c_{r}(\xi)2^{\frac{n-1}{2}})^{3+\gamma}\xi^{\frac{n}{2}}\frac{1}{2^{n+1}(\mathfrak{R}_{\min}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+\delta_{V}(\tau^{\prime\prime})2^{\frac{n}{2}})}

\displaystyle\geq
